function	script	F_tt	{
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"main")) end;
	set .@w$,(charat(strnpcinfo(4),6))+((getstrlen(strnpcinfo(4)) > 9)?(substr(strnpcinfo(4),8,9)):(charat(strnpcinfo(4),getstrlen(strnpcinfo(4))-1)));
	return .@w$;
}

-	script	入口#tt_main	-1,{
	end;
OnEnable:
	enablenpc();
	enablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;

OnStartArena:
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"n1")) {
		set .@in$, "n";
		set .@in, 1;
	} else {
		set .@in$,(compare(strnpcinfo(1),"專家"))?"e":"n";
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"4")) set .@in, 4;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"8")) set .@in, 8;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"16")) set .@in, 16;
	}
	warpwaitingpc "turbo_"+.@in$+"_"+.@in,298,161;
	donpcevent "廣播#"+.@in$+.@in+"::OnEnable";
	disablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;
	
OnInit:
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(0),"main")) end;
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2),"n1")) {
		waitingroom "單人模式",60,"單人模式#n1::OnStartArena",1,1000,10;
	} else {
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"4")) set .@in, 4;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"8")) set .@in, 8;
		if (compare(strnpcinfo(1),"16")) set .@in, 16;
		waitingroom strnpcinfo(1),60,strnpcinfo(0)+"::OnStartArena",.@in,1000,10;
	}
	enablewaitingroomevent strnpcinfo(0);
	end;
}

	close;
}

turbo_e_4,298,167,0	script	點數#tt_main	-1,15,15,{
	end;

OnTouch_:
	if (tt_point < 28999) {
		set tt_point,tt_point+2;
		warp strnpcinfo(4),59,364;
	}
	else
		warp strnpcinfo(4),59,364;
	end;
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "點數發放員#"+.@w$;
	end;
}

-	script	廣播#tt_main	-1,{
OnEnable:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "廣播#"+.@w$;
	initnpctimer;
	end;

OnTimer2000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"你現在位於等待室，可以在此檢查物品並為比賽做準備。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer7000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"距離傳送至起跑線還有30秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer10000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"請確保你已經裝備了合適的裝備與物品。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer15000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"30秒倒數即將開始。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer17000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 30 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer27000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 20 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer37000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 10 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer42000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 5 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer43000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 4 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer44000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 3 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer45000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 2 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer46000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 1 秒。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer47000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"即將傳送至起跑線。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer49000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "點數發放員#"+.@w$;
	donpcevent "管理員#"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
	end;

OnTimer50000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	donpcevent "蛇#"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
	donpcevent "狩獵#"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
	if (strnpcinfo(4) != "turbo_n_1") enablenpc "賓果#"+.@w$;
	end;

OnTimer57000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "點數發放員#"+.@w$;
	end;

OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "廣播#"+.@w$;
	end;
}

-	script	管理員#tt_main	-1,{
	function	RName;
OnEnable:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "管理員#"+.@w$;
	initnpctimer;
	end;

OnDisable:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	stopnpctimer;
	disablenpc "管理員#"+.@w$;
	end;

OnTimer7000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"歡迎來到極速賽道(Turbo Track)。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer9000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"比賽時間為15分鐘，且至少需要有一人跑完全程。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

	end;

	end;

	end;

OnTimer17000:

	end;

OnTimer18000:

	end;

OnTimer19000:

	end;

OnTimer20000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"- 2 -",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer21000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"- 1 -",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer22000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"- 0 -",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer23000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"現在！比賽開始！Go Go Go！！",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	disablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-1";
	disablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-2";
	if (strnpcinfo(4) == "turbo_n_1") set $@start_time,gettimetick(0);
	end;

OnTimer30000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"請記住這是一場15分鐘的比賽。15分鐘後，所有人將被傳送出賽道。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer83000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"剩下 14 分鐘。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer143000:

OnTimer927000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"[所有人將被傳送至等待室。]",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	mapwarp strnpcinfo(4),"turbo_room",71,89;
	disablenpc "廣播#"+.@w$;
	donpcevent "管理員#"+.@w$+"::OnDisable";
	if (strnpcinfo(4) == "turbo_n_1") {
		donpcevent "單人模式#n1::OnEnable";
	} else {
		donpcevent RName(.@w$)+"::OnEnable";
	}
	enablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-1";
	enablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-2";
	donpcevent "蛇#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";
	donpcevent "狩獵#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";
	disablenpc "賓果2#"+.@w$;
	disablenpc "獲勝助手#TBT_"+.@w$;
	enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
	enablenpc "公告員1#TBT_"+.@w$;
	enablenpc "公告員3#TBT_"+.@w$;
	enablenpc "公告員4#TBT_"+.@w$;
	enablenpc "一次性開關#"+.@w$;
	disablenpc "閃光出口_1#"+.@w$;
	disablenpc "閃光出口_2#"+.@w$;
	end;
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "管理員#"+.@w$;
	end;
	
	function	RName	{
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		.@s = ( getstrlen(.@string$) > 2 ? substr(.@string$,1,2) : charat(.@string$,getstrlen(.@string$)-1) );
		set .@rn$,((compare(strnpcinfo(4),"_e_"+.@s))?"專家模式":"一般模式")+" - "+.@s+"人";
		return .@rn$;
	}
}

	function	GetNumber;
OnTouch_:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (.@w$ == "n1") set $@end_time,gettimetick(0);
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),strcharinfo(0) +" 剛抵達了終點線！恭喜！",bc_map,"0xFFFF00";
	if (.@w$ != "n1") {
		setd "$ttnames$["+GetNumber(.@w$)+"]",strcharinfo(0);
		donpcevent "極速賽道嚮導::OnWin_"+.@w$;
	}
	warp strnpcinfo(4),384,161;
	if (.@w$ == "e4" || .@w$ == "n4" || .@w$ == "n1") {
		if (.@w$ == "e4") {
			donpcevent "獲勝助手#TBT_"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
		} else {
			donpcevent "嚮導#TBT_"+.@w$+"::OnEnable";
		}
		donpcevent "管理員#"+.@w$+"::OnDisable";
		disablenpc "廣播#"+.@w$;
	} else {
		if (.@w$ == "e8" || .@w$ == "n8") setarray .@pts, 28951,50;
		if (.@w$ == "e16" || .@w$ == "n16") setarray .@pts, 28941,60;
		if (tt_point < .@pts[0]) set tt_point,tt_point+.@pts[1];
		warp strnpcinfo(4),384,161;
		enablenpc "獲勝助手#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end2";
	}
	disablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
	end;
	
	function	GetNumber	{
		setarray .@w_n$[1], "n4","n8","n16","e4","e8","e16";
		for (set .@i, 1; .@i <= getarraysize(.@w_n$); set .@i, .@i + 1) {
			if (getarg(0) == .@w_n$[.@i]) 
				return .@i;
		}		
	}
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
	end;
}

				mes "作為獲勝者，你的獎勵";
				mes "是 40 點極速賽道點數?";
				set tt_point,tt_point+40;

				mes strcharinfo(0) +"。";

			mes "很抱歉，我不能給你";

			mes "因為這會超過你的";
			mes "最大上限。抱歉，";
			mes strcharinfo(0) +"...";
		} else {
			mes "作為獲勝者，你的獎勵";
			mes "是 "+((compare(.@w$,"8"))?"50":"40")+" 點極速賽道點數，";

			mes "你現在總共有 " + my_point + " 點極速賽道點數。";
		} 
		set tt_rank,tt_rank+1;
		next;
		if (tt_rank > $ttranks[1]) {

OnEnable:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "獲勝助手#TBT_"+.@w$;
	initnpctimer;
	end;

OnTimer4000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"這是極速賽道 "+RName(.@w$)+" 的結束廣播。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer8000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"為了遊戲的順暢進行，比賽將在大約 1 分鐘後結束。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer12000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"屆時，將會開啟一個傳送陣。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer16000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (compare(.@w$,"4")) mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"賽場內的玩家請準備好透過傳送陣離開。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer20000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (compare(.@w$,"4"))
		mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"同時，獲勝者請盡快前往領取獎勵。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	else
		mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"賽場內的玩家必須準備好進入傳送陣。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;

OnTimer24000:

	end;

OnTimer60000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (compare(.@w$,"4")) {
		enablenpc "公告員1#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "公告員3#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "公告員4#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "一次性開關#"+.@w$;
		disablenpc "閃光出口_1#"+.@w$;
		disablenpc "閃光出口_2#"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-1";
		enablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-2";
		donpcevent "蛇#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";
		donpcevent "狩獵#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";
		donpcevent RName(.@w$)+"::OnEnable";
		disablenpc "賓果2#"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
		stopnpctimer;
	} else {
		mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"我們再次感謝所有的贊助商。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	}
	end;
	
OnTimer65000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (!compare(.@w$,"4")) mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"祝您有美好的一天。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";
	end;
	
OnTimer70000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (!compare(.@w$,"4")) mapwarp strnpcinfo(4),"turbo_room",72,89;
	end;
	
OnTimer71000:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	if (!compare(.@w$,"4")) {
		disablenpc "獲勝助手#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";
		enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end2";
		enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end3";
		disablenpc "公告員1#TBT_"+.@w$;
		disablenpc "公告員3#TBT_"+.@w$;
		disablenpc "公告員4#TBT_"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "一次性開關#"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "閃光出口_1#"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "閃光出口_2#"+.@w$;
		enablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-1";
		enablenpc "禁止偷跑#"+.@w$+"-2";
		donpcevent "蛇#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";
		donpcevent "狩獵#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";
		donpcevent RName(.@w$)+"::OnEnable";
		disablenpc "賓果2#"+.@w$;
		stopnpctimer;
	}
	end;

	function	RName	{
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		.@s = ( getstrlen(.@string$) > 2 ? substr(.@string$,1,2) : charat(.@string$,getstrlen(.@string$)-1) );
		set .@rn$,((compare(strnpcinfo(4),"_e_"+.@s))?"專家模式":"一般模式")+" - "+.@s+"人";
		return .@rn$;
	}
	
OnInit:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "獲勝助手#TBT_"+.@w$;
	end;
}

turbo_n_1,384,167,1	script	嚮導#TBT_n1	47,{
	set .@hour_start,$@start_time / 10000;
	set .@min_start,(($@start_time % 10000) / 100);
	set .@sec_start,$@start_time % 100;
	set .@hour_end,$@end_time / 10000;
	set .@min_end,(($@end_time % 10000) / 100);
	set .@sec_end,$@end_time % 100;
	if (hour_start == 23 && hour_end == 0) set .@hour_end,24;
	set .@st_to_sec,((.@hour_start * 3600) + (.@min_start * 60) + (.@sec_start));
	set .@et_to_sec,((.@hour_end * 3600) + (.@min_end * 60) + (.@sec_end));
	set .@record_time,(.@et_to_sec - .@st_to_sec);
	set .@record_hour,.@record_time / 3600;
	set .@record_min,((.@record_time % 3600) / 60);
	set .@record_sec,.@record_time % 60;
	if (.@record_min < 0) {
		mes "[嚮導]";
		mes "做得好！也許你沒有";
		mes "創下任何新紀錄，但你";
		mes "跑完全程了。現在，讓";
		mes "我為你消除疲勞吧?";
		percentheal 100,100;
		next;
		mes "[嚮導]";
		if (tt_point < 28991) {
			mes "你將獲得 10 點";
			mes "極速賽道點數的獎勵?！";
			set tt_point,tt_point+10;
			set my_point,tt_point;
			mes "你現在總共有";
			mes tt_point + " 點極速賽道點數。";
		}
		else {
			mes "很抱歉，我不能給你";
			mes "任何極速賽道點數，";
			mes "因為這會超過你的";
			mes "最大上限。抱歉，";
			mes strcharinfo(0) +"...";
		}
		next;
		mes "[嚮導]";
		mes "感謝你";
		mes "參加";
		mes "極速賽道。";
		mes "你很快就會被引導";
		mes "至等待室。";
		close2;
		warp "turbo_room",72,89;
		end;
	}
	else {
		set .@topbun,(($ttranks[0] % 3600) / 60 );
		set .@topcho,$ttranks[0] % 60;
		set .@gap,$ttranks[0] - .@record_time;
		if (.@gap < 0) {
			mes "[嚮導]";
			mes "做得好！也許你沒有";
			mes "創下任何新紀錄，但你";
			mes "跑完全程了。現在，讓";
			mes "我為你消除疲勞吧?";
			percentheal 100,100;
			next;
			mes "[嚮導]";
			if (tt_point < 28991) {
				mes "你將獲得 10 點";
				mes "極速賽道點數的獎勵?！";
				set tt_point,tt_point+10;
				set my_point,tt_point;
				mes "你現在總共有";
				mes tt_point + " 點極速賽道點數。";
			}
			else {
				mes "很抱歉，我不能給你";
				mes "任何極速賽道點數，";
				mes "因為這會超過你的";
				mes "最大上限。抱歉，";
				mes strcharinfo(0) +"...";
			}
			next;
			mes "[嚮導]";
			mes "感謝你";
			mes "參加";
			mes "極速賽道。";
			mes "你很快就會被引導";
			mes "至等待室。";
			close2;
			warp "turbo_room",72,89;
			end;
		}
		else {
			mes "[嚮導]";
			mes "恭喜！";
			mes "你在單人模式中";
			mes "排名第一！";
			mes "你的名字將被登錄";
			mes "在我們的紀錄中，"+ strcharinfo(0) +"?";
			setarray $ttranks[0],.@record_time;
			setarray $ttnames$[0],strcharinfo(0);
			next;
			if (tt_point < 28961) {
				mes "[嚮導]";
				mes "既然你創下";
				mes "了新紀錄，你將";
				mes "獲得";
				mes "40 點極速賽道點數作為獎勵！";
				set tt_point,tt_point+40;
				mes "你現在總共有";
				mes tt_point + " 點極速賽道點數。";
			}
			else {
				mes "[嚮導]";
				mes "很抱歉，我不能給你";
				mes "任何極速賽道點數，";
				mes "因為這會超過你的";
				mes "最大上限。抱歉，";
				mes strcharinfo(0) +"...";
			}
			next;
			mes "[嚮導]";
			mes "感謝你";
			mes "參加";
			mes "極速賽道。";
			mes "你很快就會被引導";
			mes "至等待室。";
			close2;
			warp "turbo_room",72,89;
			end;
		}
	}
	
OnEnable:
	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	enablenpc "嚮導#TBT_"+.@w$;
	initnpctimer;
	end;

OnTimer4000:
	mapannounce strnpcinfo(4),"這是極速賽道單人模式的結束廣播。",bc_map,"0x33FF66";

	set .@w$,callfunc("F_tt");
	disablenpc "嚮導#TBT_"+.@w$;
	enablenpc "#cos_"+.@w$+"_end";

	enablenpc "公告員4#TBT_"+.@w$;

	donpcevent "狩獵#"+.@w$+"::OnReset";

OnInit:
	// This will only ever be run ONCE!
	// Unless you eradicate the variable.
	//
	// This OnInit initiallizes and sets the starting
	// data for the Turbo Track's "Record" Logs.
	// WARNING/ATTENTION: DO NOT MODIFY ANY OF THE DATA.
	// DOING SO WILL CAUSE INTERNAL PROBLEMS IN THE SCRIPT.
	if ($ttnames$[0] == "") {
		setarray $ttnames$[0],"Breezy Havana";	// Solo Mode/Time Attack
		setarray $ttnames$[1],"RS125";	// Normal 4 Player
		setarray $ttnames$[2],"Hollgrehenn";
		// Normal 8 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[3],"Antonio";	// Noomal 16 Players

		setarray $ttnames$[4],"Aragham";	// Expert 4 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[5],"Kafra Jasmine";
		// Expert 8 Players
		setarray $ttnames$[6],"Chris";	// Expert 16 Players

		setarray $ttnames$[7],"Breezy Havana";	// Honor Place 1
		setarray $ttnames$[8],"RS125";
		// Honor Place 2

		setarray $ttnames$[9],"Breezy Havana";	// Top 1
		setarray $ttnames$[10],"Nari";	// Top 2
		setarray $ttnames$[11],"Senorita Sylvia";	// Top 3
		setarray $ttnames$[12],"Joo Jahk";
		// Top 4
		setarray $ttnames$[13],"RS125";	// Top 5

		setarray $ttranks[0],999999,0,0,0,0,0;
	}
	end;

turbo_room,106,117,3	script	點數管理員#tt	833,{
	mes "[點數管理員]";
	mes "你好。";
	mes "你享受在極速賽道";
	mes "的時光嗎？";
	mes "有什麼我能";
	mes "幫忙的嗎？";
	next;
	switch(select("檢查點數:轉換點數:^660000轉換資訊^000000")) {
	case 1:
		mes "[點數管理員]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + "，";
		mes "你目前擁有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 點極速賽道點數";
		mes "與 " + arena_point + " 點競技場點數。";
		close;
	case 2:
		mes "[點數管理員]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + "，";
		mes "你目前擁有";
		mes "" + tt_point + " 點極速賽道點數";
		mes "與 " + arena_point + " 點競技場點數。";
		next;
		mes "[點數管理員]";
		mes "請從以下競技場點數轉換選項中選擇。當一次轉換超過";
		mes "10點競技場點數時，你";
		mes "只能轉換";
		mes "^4D4DFF10 的倍數^000000 的競技場點數。";
		next;
		switch(select("2 AP -> 1 TTP:4 AP -> 2 TTP:6 AP -> 3 TTP:8 AP -> 4 TTP:10 AP 或以上:取消")) {
		case 1:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28999,2,1;
		case 2:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28998,4,2;
		case 3:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28997,6,2;
		case 4:
			callsub S_ExchangePoints,28996,8,4;
		case 5:
			mes "[點數管理員]";
			mes "請輸入你希望將 10 點競技場點數轉換為極速賽道點數的次數。你可以輸入的最大值";
			mes "為 20。要取消，請輸入 '^3355FF0^000000'。";
			next;
			input .@input;
			if (.@input <= 0) {
				mes "[點數管理員]";
				mes "你已經";
				mes "取消了";
				mes "你的請求。";
				close;
			}
			else if (.@input > 20) {
				mes "[點數管理員]";
				mes "你的請求超過了";
				mes "最大限制。請";
				mes "輸入不大於 20 的數值。";
				close;
			}
			else {
				set .@want_point1,10 * .@input;
				set .@want_point,5 * .@input;
				set .@my_turbo_all,tt_point+.@want_point;
				set .@my_arena_all,arena_point-.@want_point1;
				if (.@my_turbo_all > 28999) {
					mes "[點數管理員]";
					mes "很遺憾，如果我們繼續進行點數轉換，你的極速賽道點數將會超過最大上限。在使用此服務前，請先花費一些";
					mes "極速賽道點數。謝謝。";
					close;
				}
				if (.@my_arena_all < 0) {
					mes "[點數管理員]";
					mes "很抱歉，但是你沒有";

					mes "總計 " + .@want_point1 + " 點競技場點數";
					mes "已經轉換為";
					mes "" + .@want_point +" 點極速賽道點數。";
					set arena_point,.@my_arena_all; //arena_point
					set tt_point,.@my_turbo_all;
					next;
					mes "[點數管理員]";
					mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + "，";
					mes "你現在擁有";
					mes "^00688B" + tt_point + "^000000 點極速賽道點數";
					mes "與 ^4682B4" + arena_point + "^000000 點競技場點數。";
					mes "感謝你的光臨。";

			mes "你已經";
			mes "取消了";
			mes "你的請求。";
			close;
		}

		// There is no dialog for this option.
		// It's possible it's missing, or maybe
		// the dialog was never translated. XD
		//??
		close;
	}

S_ExchangePoints:
	if (tt_point > getarg(0)) {
		mes "[點數管理員]";
		mes "很遺憾，如果我們繼續進行點數轉換，你的極速賽道點數將會超過最大上限。在使用此服務前，請先花費一些";
		mes "極速賽道點數。謝謝。";
		close;
	}
	if (arena_point >= getarg(1)) {
		mes "[點數管理員]";
		mes "如你所願，";
		mes "2 點競技場點數";
		mes "已經轉換";
		mes "為 1 點極速賽道點數。";
		set arena_point,arena_point-getarg(1);
		set tt_point,tt_point+getarg(2);
		next;
		mes "[點數管理員]";
		mes "" + strcharinfo(0) + "，";
		mes "你現在擁有";
		mes "^00688B" + tt_point + "^000000 點極速賽道點數";
		mes "與 ^4682B4" + arena_point + "^000000 點競技場點數。";
		mes "感謝你的光臨。";
		close;
	}
	else {
		mes "[點數管理員]";
		mes "很抱歉，但是你沒有足夠的競技場點數。你需要";
		mes "至少 2 點競技場點數才能";
		mes "使用此服務。";
		close;
	}
}

				mes .@mount$+"，你將會獲得一張";
				mes "大嘴鳥免費騎乘卷，";
				mes "以便之後取回你的"+.@mount$+"。";
			} else {
				set .@mount$,(BaseJob == Job_Knight)?"Dragon":"Gryphon";
				mes "[助手]";
				mes "極速賽道場內禁止";
				mes "騎乘"+.@mount$+"。";
				mes "請解除騎乘你的";
				mes .@mount$+"。你可以稍後在外面";
				mes "免費取回。";
			}
			close2;
			warp "alde_gld",183,199;
			end;
		} else {
			if (!(eaclass()&EAJL_THIRD)) {
				setriding 0;
				getitem 7310,1; //Free_Peco_Ticket
			} else {
				if (BaseJob == Job_Knight)
					setdragon 0;
				else
					setriding 0;
			}
			warp "turbo_room",100,65;
		}
	} else if ((Class == Job_Ranger || Class == Job_Ranger_T || Class == Job_Baby_Ranger) && checkoption(Option_Wugrider)) {
		if (MaxWeight-Weight < 20000) {
			mes "[助手]";
			mes "極速賽道場內禁止";
			mes "騎乘狼。";
			mes "請解除騎乘狼。";
			close2;
			warp "alde_gld",183,199;
			end;
		} else {
			setoption Option_Wugrider,0;
			warp "turbo_room",100,65;
		}
	} else if ((Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == Job_Baby_Mechanic) && checkmadogear()) {
		if (MaxWeight-Weight < 20000) {
			mes "[助手]";
			mes "極速賽道場內禁止";
			mes "搭乘魔導甲。";
			mes "請解除搭乘你的";
			mes "魔導甲。你可以稍後在外面";
			mes "免費取回。";
			close2;
			warp "alde_gld",183,199;
			end;
		} else {
			setmadogear 0;
			warp "turbo_room",100,65;
		}
	} else
		warp "turbo_room",100,65;
	end;
}

alde_gld,186,199,3	script	極速賽道嚮導#Entran	845,{
	mes "[嚮導]";
	mes "歡迎來到";
	mes "艾爾帕蘭";
	mes "極速賽道?";
	next;
	mes "[嚮導]";
	if (checkre(0))
		mes "極速賽道場內禁止使用大嘴鳥、大嘴鳥(進階)、龍、獅鷲獸、狼以及魔導甲。";
	else
		mes "極速賽道場內禁止使用大嘴鳥。";
	mes "但任何騎乘大嘴鳥的人都會在極速賽道入口處獲得一張大嘴鳥免費騎乘卷並自動解除騎乘。";
	next;
	mes "[嚮導]";
	mes "那麼，";
	mes "祝你在極速賽道";
	mes "玩得愉快?！";
	close;
}

-	script	::MountManager_turbo	-1,{
	set .@n$, "["+strnpcinfo(1)+"]";
	mes .@n$;
	if (ismounting()) {
		mes "請先解除騎乘狀態。";
		mes "然後再和我說話。";
		close;
	}
	if (BaseJob == Job_Knight || BaseJob == Job_Crusader) {
		if (!(eaclass()&EAJL_THIRD)) {
			if (BaseJob == Job_Knight) {
				set .@zeny,2500;
				set .@zeny$,"2,500";
				set .@mount$,"Peco Peco";
			} else {
				set .@zeny,3500;
				set .@zeny$,"3,500";
				set .@mount$,"Grand Peco";
			}
			set .@skill,63;
			set .@skill$,"騎乘術";
			set .@riding, checkriding();
			set .@i,1;
		} else {
			if (BaseJob == Job_Knight) {
				set .@mount$,"Dragon";
				set .@skill,63;
				set .@skill$,"龍駕馭";
				set .@riding, checkdragon();
				set .@i,2;
			} else {
				set .@mount$,"Gryphon";
				set .@skill,63;
				set .@skill$,"騎乘術";
				set .@riding, checkriding();
				set .@i,1;
			}
		}
	} else if (Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == Job_Baby_Mechanic) {
		set .@mount$,"Magic Gear";
		set .@skill,2255;
		set .@skill$,"魔導甲執照";
		set .@riding, checkmadogear();
		set .@i,3;
	} else {
		mes "感謝你";
		mes "蒞臨艾爾帕蘭的";
		mes "極速賽道?";
		close;
	}
	mes "歡迎，你想";
	mes "取回你的"+.@mount$+"嗎？";
	if (.@zeny) {
		mes "請出示你的大嘴鳥";
		mes "免費騎乘卷。你";
		mes "也可以支付";
		mes .@zeny$+" zeny 的租借費用。";
	}
	next;
	switch(select("好:取消")) {
	case 1:
		if (getskilllv(.@skill) == 0) {
			mes .@n$;
			mes "很抱歉，但你";
			mes "不符合這項服務的資格。";
			mes "請先去學習";
			mes .@skill$+" 技能。";
			close;
		} else {
			if (.@riding) {
				mes .@n$;
				mes "你已經";
				mes "處於";
				mes .@mount$+" 騎乘狀態。";
				mes "謝謝?";
				close;
			} else {
				if (.@zeny) {
					if (countitem(7310) < 1 && Zeny < .@zeny) {
						mes .@n$;
						mes "很抱歉，但你";
						mes "沒有大嘴鳥";
						mes "免費騎乘卷或 "+.@zeny$+" zeny";
						mes "來使用租借服務。";
						close;
					} else {
						if (countitem(7310) > 0)
							delitem 7310,1; //Free_Peco_Ticket
						else
							set Zeny, Zeny-.@zeny;
					}
				}
				switch(.@i) {
					case 1: setriding; break;
					case 2: setdragon; break;
					case 3: setmadogear; break;
				}
				mes .@n$;
				mes "感謝你的";
				mes "光臨?";
				close;
			}
		}
	case 2:
		mes .@n$;
		mes "你要回去";
		mes "極速賽道";
		mes "比賽了嗎？";
		mes "祝你好運！";
		close;
	}
}
